Batch scope and revisions\n\nThe batch file lists *artwork*, not pages. A page that has been re-supplied appears more\nthan once, once per `revision`, under the same `page_id`. Batch scope and revisions\n\nThe batch file lists *artwork*, not pages. A page that has been re-supplied appears more\nthan once, once per `revision`, under the same `page_id`. Only the highest-numbered\nrevision of a `page_id` is live artwork and goes to press; every lower revision is\nsuperseded and is not audited. The rows are not in revision order. Every figure reported\nfor the batch counts live pages only.\n\n## 1. Sections, binding and stock\n\nEach page belongs to a section (`kit_sections.csv`), and the section fixes both the binding\nand the paper stock that section is printed on. The limits in §2–§5 are properties of the\n**stock** (`stock_profiles.csv`), not of the kit as a whole, so the same measurement can be\na breach in one section and clear in another.\n\nEvery limit in this standard is inclusive: a measurement exactly equal to its limit meets\nthe limit. Only a measurement strictly worse than the limit is a breach.\n\n## 2. Effective resolution\n\nArtwork is supplied at `artwork_dpi` and placed on the page at `placement_scale_pct`. What\nactually prints is the effective resolution:\n\n    effective_dpi = floor(artwork_dpi * 100 / placement_scale_pct)\n\nA page placed larger than 100% therefore resolves *lower* than its supplied figure, and one\nplaced smaller resolves higher. Below the stock's `min_dpi` it is `DPI_TOO_LOW`.\n\n## 3. Safe margin\n\n`margin_in` must be at least the stock's `min_margin_in` — the clear distance between any\ntext or graphic element and the trim edge. Below it is `MARGIN_VIOLATION`.\n\n## 4. Full-bleed pages: one exemption, one obligation\n\nA page tagged `full_bleed_design` is **exempt from §3**. Its art is meant to run to the trim\nedge, so a thin margin on a full-bleed page is by design; flagging it is the commonest false\npositive in this audit.\n\n**The exemption is limited to saddle-stitched sections.** Perfect binding glues the spine\nedge, so a perfect-bound page has no bleed on that edge and its live copy still has to clear\nthe trim: a full-bleed page in a `perfect_bound` section is judged under §3 exactly like any\nother page. Only a full-bleed page in a `saddle_stitch` section is released from §3.\n\nEvery full-bleed page, whatever its binding, carries an obligation no other page carries: it\nmust be supplied with `bleed_in` of at least the stock's `min_bleed_in`, or the trim eats\ninto the art. Below it is `BLEED_SHORT`. `bleed_in` on a page that is not full-bleed is not\nassessed.\n\n## 5. Ink coverage\n\n`total_ink_pct` must not exceed the stock's `max_ink_pct`. Above it is `INK_OVER`.\n\n## 6. Required elements\n\nEach page type carries a minimum number of required elements:\n\n| page_type | minimum |\n| --- | --- |\n| `cover` | 1 |\n| `parent_guide` | 2 |\n| `map` | 4 |\n| `activity` | 4 |\n| `mission_cards` | 6 |\n| `bingo` | 9 |\n| `badge_certificate` | 4 |\n\n**Amendment Rev B.** A saddle-stitched section is trimmed harder at the outer edge, so its\ndetachable-element pages have to be supplied with spares. In a section bound\n`saddle_stitch`, the minimum for `mission_cards` and for `badge_certificate` is\n2 higher than the table above. No other page type and no other binding is\naffected.\n\nA page below its own minimum is `ELEMENT_COUNT_SHORT`. A page's shortfall is its minimum\nless its `element_count`; a page at or above its minimum has a shortfall of zero.\n\n## 7. Findings\n\n`finding` is one or more of `DPI_TOO_LOW`, `MARGIN_VIOLATION`, `BLEED_SHORT`, `INK_OVER`,\n`ELEMENT_COUNT_SHORT`, joined with `|`, or `none` for a page that breaches nothing.\n\n## 8. Imposition\n\nA page carrying `ELEMENT_COUNT_SHORT` cannot go to press as it stands: it is **held back**\nfor re-supply and does not sit in the imposition. What has to impose is the section's live\npage count *less* the pages held back.\n\nThat imposed count has to fit the section's binding:\n\n| binding | imposed page count must be |\n| --- | --- |\n| `saddle_stitch` | a multiple of 4 |\n| `perfect_bound` | a multiple of 2 |\n\nA section whose imposed page count does not fit its binding is `IMPOSITION_INVALID` and has\nto be repaginated before the run; the pages it needs is the number that would carry it up to\nthe next multiple. A section that fits is `none`.\n\n§8 is a property of the section, not of any page in it: an `IMPOSITION_INVALID` section is\nnot a finding against its pages, and a section with no flagged pages at all can still fail\nto impose.","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":106,"totalLines":106,"truncated":false}},"title":"app/input/activity_kit_print_standard.md","time":{"start":1787198475522,"end":1787198475723}},"id":"prt_01d548ce4001Ps2qIrmqvpk8pW","sessionID":"ses_fe2ab8b3bffejOe7xVE5zFpuXz","messageID":"msg_01d548730001IiONMOQoLh05RY"}}

Let me break it down.\n\n## Understanding the inputs\n\n### 1. `activity_kit_print_standard.md` (PRINT-KIT-2)\n\nKey points:\n- §0 Batch scope and revisions: A page that has been re-supplied appears more than once, once per `revision`, under the same `page_id`. Only the highest-numbered revision of a `page_id` is live artwork and goes to press; every lower revision is superseded and is not audited. The rows are not in revision order. Every figure reported for the batch counts live pages only.\n\n- §1 Sections, binding and stock: Each page belongs to a section. The section fixes binding and stock. The limits in §2–§5 are properties of the **stock**, not of the kit. Every limit is inclusive: a measurement exactly equal to its limit meets the limit. Only a measurement strictly worse than the limit is a breach.\n\n- §2 Effective resolution:\n  - effective_dpi = floor(artwork_dpi * 100 / placement_scale_pct)\n  - Below the stock's `min_dpi` it is `DPI_TOO_LOW`.\n\n- §3 Safe margin: `margin_in` must be at least the stock's `min_margin_in`. Below it is `MARGIN_VIOLATION`.\n\n- §4 Full-bleed pages:\n  - A page tagged `full_bleed_design` is **exempt from §3**.\n  - **The exemption is limited to saddle-stitched sections.** Perfect binding glues the spine edge, so a full-bleed page in a `perfect_bound` section is judged under §3 exactly like any other page. Only a full-bleed page in a `saddle_stitch` section is released from §3.\n  - Every full-bleed page, whatever its binding, carries an obligation: it must be supplied with `bleed_in` of at least the stock's `min_bleed_in`. Below it is `BLEED_SHORT`. `bleed_in` on a page that is not full-bleed is not assessed.\n\n- §5 Ink coverage: `total_ink_pct` must not exceed the stock's `max_ink_pct`. Above it is `INK_OVER`.\n\n- §6 Required elements:\n  - Each page type carries a minimum number of required elements:\n    - cover: 1\n    - parent_guide: 2\n    - map: 4\n    - activity: 4\n    - mission_cards: 6\n    - bingo: 9\n    - badge_certificate: 4\n  - **Amendment Rev B.** A saddle-stitched section is trimmed harder at the outer edge, so its detachable-element pages have to be supplied with spares. In a section bound `saddle_stitch`, the minimum for `mission_cards` and for `badge_certificate` is 2 higher than the table above. No other page type and no other binding is affected.\n  - A page below its own minimum is `ELEMENT_COUNT_SHORT`. A page's shortfall is its minimum less its `element_count`; a page at or above its minimum has a shortfall of zero.\n\n- §7 Findings: `finding` is one or more of `DPI_TOO_LOW`, `MARGIN_VIOLATION`, `BLEED_SHORT`, `INK_OVER`, `ELEMENT_COUNT_SHORT`, joined with `|`, or `none`.\n\n- §8 Imposition:\n  - A page carrying `ELEMENT_COUNT_SHORT` is **held back** for re-supply and does not sit in the imposition. What has to impose is the section's live page count *less* the pages held back.\n  - The imposed count must fit the binding:\n    - saddle_stitch: a multiple of 4\n    - perfect_bound: a multiple of 2\n  - A section whose imposed page count does not fit its binding is `IMPOSITION_INVALID` and has to be repaginated; the pages it needs is the number that would carry it up to the next multiple. A section that fits is `none`.\n  - §8 is a property of the section, not of any page in it.\n\n### 2. `kit_sections.csv`\n- SEC-01, Front matter, perfect_bound, BRD-250\n- SEC-02, Explorer trail, saddle_stitch, CST-100\n- SEC-03, Mission deck, saddle_stitch, BRD-250\n- SEC-04, Station games, perfect_bound, CST-100\n- SEC-05, Grown-up pack, saddle_stitch, UNC-120\n- SEC-06, Rewards, perfect_bound, UNC-120\n- SEC-07, Trail extras, saddle_stitch, CST-100\n- SEC-08, Take-home pack, perfect_bound, BRD-250\n\n### 3. `stock_profiles.csv`\n- CST-100, Coated silk 100gsm, min_dpi=300, min_margin_in=0.125, max_ink_pct=280, min_bleed_in=0.125\n- UNC-120, Uncoated offset 120gsm, min_dpi=240, min_margin_in=0.1875, max_ink_pct=240, min_bleed_in=0.25\n- BRD-250, Board 250gsm, min_dpi=260, min_margin_in=0.25, max_ink_pct=300, min_bleed_in=0.1875\n\n### 4. `activity_kit_pages.csv`\nThere are 42 data rows (43 lines including header, so 42 rows). I'll use the csv module.\n\nLet me write it.\n\n```python\nimport csv\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom decimal import Decimal\n\nIN_DIR = \"/app/input\"\nOUT_DIR = \"/app\"\n\n# Load stock profiles\nstocks = {}\nwith open(os.path.join(IN_DIR, \"stock_profiles.csv\")) as f:\n    for row in csv.DictReader(f):\n        stocks[row[\"stock_code\"]] = {\n            \"stock_name\": row[\"stock_name\"],\n            \"min_dpi\": int(row[\"min_dpi\"]),\n            \"min_margin_in\": Decimal(row[\"min_margin_in\"]),\n            \"max_ink_pct\": int(row[\"max_ink_pct\"]),\n            \"min_bleed_in\": Decimal(row[\"min_bleed_in\"]),\n        }\n\n# Load sections\nsections = {}\nsection_order = []\nwith open(os.path.join(IN_DIR, \"kit_sections.csv\")) as f:\n    for row in csv.DictReader(f):\n        sections[row[\"section_id\"]] = {\n            \"section_name\": row[\"section_name\"],\n            \"binding\": row[\"binding\"],\n            \"stock_code\": row[\"stock_code\"],\n        }\n        section_order.append(row[\"section_id\"])\n\n# Load pages\nall_rows = []\nwith open(os.path.join(IN_DIR, \"activity_kit_pages.csv\")) as f:\n    for row in csv.DictReader(f):\n        all_rows.append({\n            \"artwork_id\": row[\"artwork_id\"],\n            \"page_id\": row[\"page_id\"],\n            \"revision\": int(row[\"revision\"]),\n            \"page_name\": row[\"page_name\"],\n            \"page_type\": row[\"page_type\"],\n            \"section_id\": row[\"section_id\"],\n            \"artwork_dpi\": int(row[\"artwork_dpi\"]),\n            \"placement_scale_pct\": int(row[\"placement_scale_pct\"]),\n            \"margin_in\": Decimal(row[\"margin_in\"]),\n            \"full_bleed\": row[\"full_bleed_design\"] == \"True\",\n            \"bleed_in\": Decimal(row[\"bleed_in\"]),\n            \"total_ink_pct\": int(row[\"total_ink_pct\"]),\n            \"element_count\": int(row[\"element_count\"]),\n        })\n\n# Determine live rows (highest revision per page_id)\nby_page = {}\nfor r in all_rows:\n    by_page.setdefault(r[\"page_id\"], []).append(r)\n\nlive = {}\nsuperseded = []\nfor pid, rows in by_page.items():\n    rows.sort(key=lambda x: x[\"revision\"])\n    live_row = rows[-1]\n    live[pid] = live_row\n    for r in rows[:-1]:\n        superseded.append(r)\n\nsuperseded_count = len(superseded)\n\n# Element minimums\nbase_min = {\n    \"cover\": 1,\n    \"parent_guide\": 2,\n    \"map\": 4,\n    \"activity\": 4,\n    \"mission_cards\": 6,\n    \"bingo\": 9,\n    \"badge_certificate\": 4,\n}\n\nFINDING_ORDER = [\"DPI_TOO_LOW\", \"MARGIN_VIOLATION\", \"BLEED_SHORT\", \"INK_OVER\", \"ELEMENT_COUNT_SHORT\"]\n\ndef element_min(page_type, binding):\n    m = base_min[page_type]\n    if binding == \"saddle_stitch\" and page_type in (\"mission_cards\", \"badge_certificate\"):\n        m += 2\n    return m\n\n# Audit each live page\npage_findings = {}  # page_id -> dict with findings and details\npage_ids_sorted = sorted(live.keys(), key=lambda p: int(p.split(\"-\")[1]))\n\nfor pid in page_ids_sorted:\n    r = live[pid]\n    sec = sections[r[\"section_id\"]]\n    stock = stocks[sec[\"stock_code\"]]\n    binding = sec[\"binding\"]\n    \n    findings = []\n    detail = {}\n    \n    # effective dpi\n    eff = (r[\"artwork_dpi\"] * 100) // r[\"placement_scale_pct\"]\n    if eff < stock[\"min_dpi\"]:\n        findings.append(\"DPI_TOO_LOW\")\n        detail[\"DPI_TOO_LOW\"] = (eff, stock[\"min_dpi\"])\n    \n    # margin (unless full-bleed + saddle_stitch)\n    exempt_margin = r[\"full_bleed\"] and binding == \"saddle_stitch\"\n    if not exempt_margin:\n        if r[\"margin_in\"] < stock[\"min_margin_in\"]:\n            findings.append(\"MARGIN_VIOLATION\")\n            detail[\"MARGIN_VIOLATION\"] = (r[\"margin_in\"], stock[\"min_margin_in\"])\n    \n    # bleed (only if full_bleed)\n    if r[\"full_bleed\"]:\n        if r[\"bleed_in\"] < stock[\"min_bleed_in\"]:\n            findings.append(\"BLEED_SHORT\")\n            detail[\"BLEED_SHORT\"] = (r[\"bleed_in\"], stock[\"min_bleed_in\"])\n    \n    # ink\n    if r[\"total_ink_pct\"] > stock[\"max_ink_pct\"]:\n        findings.append(\"INK_OVER\")\n        detail[\"INK_OVER\"] = (r[\"total_ink_pct\"], stock[\"max_ink_pct\"])\n    \n    # elements\n    emin = element_min(r[\"page_type\"], binding)\n    if r[\"element_count\"] < emin:\n        findings.append(\"ELEMENT_COUNT_SHORT\")\n        detail[\"ELEMENT_COUNT_SHORT\"] = (r[\"element_count\"], emin, emin - r[\"element_count\"])\n    \n    # order\n    findings = [f for f in FINDING_ORDER if f in findings]\n    finding_str = \"|\".join(findings) if findings else \"none\"\n    \n    page_findings[pid] = {\n        \"row\": r,\n        \"section\": sec,\n        \"stock\": stock,\n        \"effective_dpi\": eff,\n        \"exempt_margin\": exempt_margin,\n        \"element_min\": emin,\n        \"findings\": findings,\n        \"finding_str\": finding_str,\n        \"detail\": detail,\n    }\n```\n\nThen compute section summaries, write CSVs, results.json, and print verification.\n\nLet me write the section summary computation:\n\n```python\nsection_summary = []\nfor sid in section_order:\n    pages = [pid for pid in page_ids_sorted if live[pid][\"section_id\"] == sid]\n    live_count = len(pages)\n    held_back = sum(1 for pid in pages if \"ELEMENT_COUNT_SHORT\" in page_findings[pid][\"findings\"])\n    flagged = sum(1 for pid in pages if page_findings[pid][\"findings\"])\n    imposed = live_count - held_back\n    binding = sections[sid][\"binding\"]\n    if binding == \"saddle_stitch\":\n        mod = 4\n    else:\n        mod = 2\n No other page type or\nbinding is affected.\n\n## Superseded artwork and what the re-supply changes\n\nSix pages were re-supplied.
